===== FILE: .github/workflows/deploy.yml =====
name: Deploy Astro site to Pages

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]

  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: write
  pages: write
  id-token: write

concurrency:
  group: "pages"
  cancel-in-progress: false

env:
  BUILD_PATH: "."

jobs:
  build:
    name: Build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - name: Detect package manager
        id: detect-package-manager
        run: |
          if [ -f "${{ github.workspace }}/yarn.lock" ]; then
            echo "manager=yarn" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
            echo "command=install" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
            echo "runner=yarn" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
            echo "lockfile=yarn.lock" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
            exit 0
          elif [ -f "${{ github.workspace }}/package.json" ]; then
            echo "manager=npm" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
            echo "command=ci" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
            echo "runner=npx --no-install" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
            echo "lockfile=package-lock.json" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
            exit 0
          else
            echo "Unable to determine package manager"
            exit 1
          fi

      - name: Setup Node
        uses: actions/setup-node@v7
        with:
          node-version: "24"
          cache: ${{ steps.detect-package-manager.outputs.manager }}
          cache-dependency-path: ${{ env.BUILD_PATH }}/${{ steps.detect-package-manager.outputs.lockfile }}

      - name: Setup Pages
        id: pages
        uses: actions/configure-pages@v6

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: ${{ steps.detect-package-manager.outputs.manager }} ${{ steps.detect-package-manager.outputs.command }}
        working-directory: ${{ env.BUILD_PATH }}

      - name: Build with Astro
        run: |
          ${{ steps.detect-package-manager.outputs.runner }} astro build \
            --site "${{ steps.pages.outputs.origin }}" \
            --base "${{ steps.pages.outputs.base_path }}"
        working-directory: ${{ env.BUILD_PATH }}

      - name: Upload artifact
        uses: actions/upload-pages-artifact@v5
        with:
          path: ${{ env.BUILD_PATH }}/dist

  deploy:
    environment:
      name: github-pages
      url: ${{ steps.deployment.outputs.page_url }}
    needs: build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    name: Deploy
    steps:
      - name: Deploy to GitHub Pages
        id: deployment
        uses: actions/deploy-pages@v5
===== FILE: .github/workflows/dump.yml =====
name: Dump repo

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      max_kb:
        description: Batas ukuran per file (KB)
        default: '200'

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  dump:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Gabungkan isi repo
        env:
          MAX_KB: ${{ inputs.max_kb }}
        run: |
          OUT=dump.txt
          MAX=$(( ${MAX_KB:-200} * 1024 ))
          SKIP='(^|/)(package-lock\.json|pnpm-lock\.yaml|yarn\.lock|bun\.lockb?)$|^src/content/anime/|^src/data/anime/|^src/data/actors/|^src/data/genres\.json$|^src/data/studios\.json$'

          {
            echo "# Repo: $GITHUB_REPOSITORY"
            echo "# Commit: $GITHUB_SHA"
            echo
            echo "## Struktur file"
            git ls-files | grep -Ev "$SKIP"
            echo
            echo "## Isi file"
          } > "$OUT"

          : > skipped.txt

          git ls-files -z | while IFS= read -r -d '' f; do
            if echo "$f" | grep -Eq "$SKIP"; then continue; fi
            [ -f "$f" ] || continue

            if ! grep -Iq . "$f"; then
              echo "biner atau kosong: $f" >> skipped.txt
              continue
            fi

            size=$(wc -c < "$f")
            if [ "$size" -gt "$MAX" ]; then
              echo "terlalu besar ($size byte): $f" >> skipped.txt
              continue
            fi

            {
              echo
              echo "===== FILE: $f ====="
              cat "$f"
            } >> "$OUT"
          done

          {
            echo
            echo "## File yang dilewati"
            cat skipped.txt
          } >> "$OUT"

          {
            echo "### Dump selesai"
            echo "Ukuran: $(wc -c < "$OUT") byte"
            echo "Dilewati: $(wc -l < skipped.txt) file"
          } >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"

      - name: Simpan hasil ke branch dump
        run: |
          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"

          git checkout --orphan dump
          git rm -rf --quiet .
          git add dump.txt
          git commit -m "Dump repo @ ${GITHUB_SHA::7}"
          git push --force origin dump

          echo "File: https://github.com/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY/blob/dump/dump.txt" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
===== FILE: .github/workflows/lock.yml =====
name: Generate Package Lock

on:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  lock:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - name: Setup Node
        uses: actions/setup-node@v7
        with:
          node-version: 24

      - name: Generate lock file
        run: npm install --package-lock-only

      - name: Commit & push
        run: |
          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
          git config user.email "github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
          git add package-lock.json
          if git diff --staged --quiet; then
            echo "Lock file tidak berubah, skip commit."
          else
            git commit -m "chore: generate package-lock.json"
            git push
          fi

===== FILE: astro.config.mjs =====
import { defineConfig, fontProviders, svgoOptimizer } from 'astro/config';
import sitemap from '@astrojs/sitemap';

export default defineConfig({
  site: 'https://orchixs.github.io',
  trailingSlash: 'always',
  fonts: [
    {
      provider: fontProviders.local(),
      name: "Montserrat",
      cssVariable: "--font-montserrat",
      options: {
        variants: [
          {
            weight: "100 900",
            style: "normal",
            src: ["./src/assets/fonts/Montserrat.woff2"],
          },
        ],
      },
    },
    {
      provider: fontProviders.local(),
      name: "Inter",
      cssVariable: "--font-inter",
      options: {
        variants: [
          {
            weight: "100 900",
            style: "normal",
            src: ["./src/assets/fonts/Inter.woff2"],
          },
        ],
      },
    },
    {
      provider: fontProviders.local(),
      name: "FiraCode",
      cssVariable: "--font-firaCode",
      options: {
        variants: [
          {
            weight: "100 900",
            style: "normal",
            src: ["./src/assets/fonts/FiraCode.woff2"],
          },
        ],
      },
    },
  ],
  integrations: [
    sitemap({
      filter: (page) => {
        if (page.includes('/search/')) return false;
        if (page.includes('/404/')) return false;
        if (page.includes('/disclaimer/')) return false;
        if (/\/\d+\/$/.test(page)) return false;

        return true;
      },
      changefreq: 'weekly',
      priority: 0.7,
    }),
  ],
  experimental: {
    incrementalBuild: true,
    svgOptimizer: svgoOptimizer(),
  },
});

import { ADS } from '~/constants';

type Format = 'leaderboard' | 'rectangle' | 'skyscraper' | 'inline';

interface Props {
  format?: Format;
  id?: string;
  code?: string;
  label?: string;
}

const {
  format = 'inline',
  id,
  code = '',
  label = 'Slot Iklan',
} = Astro.props;

const dims: Record<Format, string> = {
  leaderboard: '728 × 90',
  rectangle: '300 × 250',
  skyscraper: '300 × 600',
  inline: 'Responsive',
};

const hasCode = code.trim().length > 0;
const showPlaceholder = !hasCode && ADS.showPlaceholder;
---

{hasCode ? (
  <div
    class:list={['ad-slot', 'ad-slot-filled', `ad-slot-${format}`]}
    id={id}
    data-ad-slot={format}
    aria-label="Iklan"
    set:html={code}
  />
) : showPlaceholder ? (
  <div
    class:list={['ad-slot', 'ad-slot-placeholder', `ad-slot-${format}`]}
    id={id}
    data-ad-slot={format}
    aria-label="Slot iklan"
  >
    <span class="ad-slot-label">{label}</span>
    <span class="ad-slot-dims">{dims[format]}</span>
  </div>
) : null}

import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';
import bannerData from '~/data/banner.json';

type BannerType = 'info' | 'warning' | 'success' | 'danger';

interface BannerLink {
  href: string;
  label: string;
}

interface Banner {
  id: string;
  active?: boolean;
  type?: BannerType;
  message: string;
  link?: BannerLink;
  dismissible?: boolean;
}

interface Props {
  class?: string;
}

const { class: className } = Astro.props;

const banners = ((bannerData as { banners?: Banner[] }).banners ?? []).filter(
  (b) => b.active !== false && b.message
);

if (banners.length === 0) return null;
---

<div class:list={['site-banner-stack', className]} data-site-banner-stack>
  {banners.map((b) => {
    const type = b.type ?? 'info';
    const dismissible = b.dismissible !== false;

    return (
      <div
        class:list={['site-banner', `site-banner--${type}`]}
        data-site-banner
        data-banner-id={b.id}
        data-dismissible={dismissible ? '1' : '0'}
        role={type === 'danger' || type === 'warning' ? 'alert' : 'status'}
      >
        <div class="site-banner__inner">
          <p class="site-banner__message">
            <span>{b.message}</span>
            {b.link && (
              <>
                {' '}
                <a class="site-banner__link" href={b.link.href}>
                  {b.link.label}
                </a>
              </>
            )}
          </p>

          {dismissible && (
            <button
              type="button"
              class="site-banner__close"
              data-banner-close
              aria-label="Tutup pengumuman"
            >
              <Icon name="close" size={14} />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  })}
</div>

<script>
  (function () {
    const STORAGE_KEY = 'qimochi:banner-dismissed';

    function loadDismissed(): Set<string> {
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        if (!raw) return new Set();
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return new Set();
        return new Set(parsed.filter((x) => typeof x === 'string'));
      } catch {
        return new Set();
      }
    }

    function saveDismissed(ids: Set<string>): void {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify([...ids]));
      } catch {}
    }

    function init(): void {
      const stack = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-site-banner-stack]');
      if (!stack) return;

      const dismissed = loadDismissed();
      const items = stack.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-site-banner]');

      items.forEach((el) => {
        const id = el.dataset.bannerId ?? '';
        const dismissible = el.dataset.dismissible === '1';

        if (id && dismissed.has(id)) {
          el.remove();
          return;
        }

        if (!dismissible || !id) return;

        const btn = el.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-banner-close]');
        if (!btn) return;

        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          dismissed.add(id);
          saveDismissed(dismissed);
          el.remove();

          if (stack.querySelectorAll('[data-site-banner]').length === 0) {
            stack.remove();
          }
        });
      });

      if (stack.querySelectorAll('[data-site-banner]').length === 0) {
        stack.remove();
      }
    }

    init();
    document.addEventListener('astro:page-load', init);
  })();
</script>

<style>
  .site-banner-stack {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    width: 100%;
  }

  .site-banner {

    cursor: pointer;
  }

  .toolbar-select:focus {
    outline: none;

  }

  .comment-avatar {

    object-fit: cover;

    min-width: 0;

  .comment-name {
    font-size: var(--fs-sm);

    color: var(--text-primary);
  }

  .comment-role {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    padding: 1px 5px;
    background: var(--gold);
    color: #1a1a1a;
    font-size: 0.5625rem;
    font-weight: var(--fw-bold);
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    line-height: 1;
    border-radius: var(--r-sm);
  }

  .comment-role[hidden] {
    display: none;
  }

  .comment-submeta {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--s-1);
  }

  .comment-time {
    font-size: var(--fs-xs);
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }

  .comment-edited {
    font-size: var(--fs-xs);
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-style: italic;
  }

  .comment-edited[hidden] {
    display: none;
  }

  .comment-badge {
    margin-left: auto;
    padding: 2px 6px;
    font-size: 0.625rem;
    font-weight: var(--fw-semibold);
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    text-transform: uppercase;

    flex-shrink: 0;

  .comment-badge[hidden] {

  .comment-badge.is-pending {

  }

  .comment-body {

    overflow-wrap: anywhere;

    opacity: 0.6;
  }

  .comment-body :global(code) {
    font-family: var(--font-mono);
    font-size: 0.9em;
    padding: 0.1em 0.35em;
    background: var(--bg-surface);

  .comment-body :global(a) {

  }

  .comment-body :global(.mention) {
    color: var(--accent);
    font-weight: var(--fw-semibold);

  }

  .comment-body :global(.spoiler) {

    user-select: none;

    transition: all var(--t-fast);
  }

  .comment-body :global(.spoiler.revealed) {
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    user-select: auto;
  }

  .comment-edit {

  .comment-edit[hidden] {

  }

  .comment-edit-input {

  .btn-mini {

  .btn-mini:hover:not(:disabled) {
    border-color: var(--accent);
    color: var(--accent);
  }

  .btn-mini:disabled {
    opacity: 0.5;
    cursor: not-allowed;
  }

  .btn-mini-primary {
    background: var(--accent);
    color: var(--accent-text);
    border-color: var(--accent);
  }

  .btn-mini-primary:hover:not(:disabled) {

    border-color: var(--accent-hover);
    color: var(--accent-text);
  }

  .comment-actions {

    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }

  .comment-action-btn {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--s-1);

    background: none;

  .comment-action-btn[hidden] {

  }

  .comment-action-btn.is-liked {

  }

  .like-icon {

    font-size: 1rem;

  }

  .comment-action-btn.is-liked .like-icon {
    transform: scale(1.15);
  }

  .like-count {
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }

  .like-count[hidden] {
    display: none;
  }

  .comment-action-danger:hover {
    color: var(--danger);
  }

  .comment-action-report:hover {
    color: var(--warning);
  }

  .comment-replies {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--s-3);
    list-style: none;
    padding: 0;
    margin: var(--s-2) 0 0 var(--s-6);
    padding-left: var(--s-3);
    border-left: 2px solid var(--border);
  }

  .comment-replies[hidden] {
    display: none;
  }

  .load-more-wrap {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    padding-block: var(--s-2);
  }

  .load-more-wrap[hidden] {
    display: none;
  }

  .load-more-btn {
    padding: var(--s-2) var(--s-5);
    background: transparent;
    border: 1px solid var(--border);
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    font-size: var(--fs-xs);
    font-weight: var(--fw-semibold);
    letter-spacing: 0.05em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all var(--t-fast);
  }

  .load-more-btn:hover {
    border-color: var(--accent);
    color: var(--accent);
  }
</style>

<script>
  interface AuthUser {
    sub: string;
    name: string;
    email: string;
    picture: string | null;
  }

  interface CommentItem {
    id: string;
    parentId: string | null;
    body: string;
    status: string;
    createdAt: number;
    editedAt: number | null;
    likes: number;
    isLiked: boolean;
    isOwn: boolean;
    isAdmin: boolean;
    canEdit: boolean;
    user: { sub: string; name: string; picture: string | null };
  }

  type SortKey = 'oldest' | 'newest' | 'popular';

  (function () {
    const CACHE_KEY = 'qimochi:user-cache';
    const SORT_KEY = 'qimochi:comment-sort';
    const COOLDOWN_SEC = 15;
    const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

    const root = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-comments]');
    if (!root) return;

    const SLUG = root.dataset.slug || '';
    const API = (root.dataset.api || '').replace(/\/$/, '');
    const SITE_KEY = root.dataset.sitekey || '';

    const countEl = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-count]')!;
    const skeletonBox = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-auth-skeleton]')!;
    const loginBox = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-login-box]')!;
    const gsiSlot = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-gsi-slot]')!;
    const userBox = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-user-box]')!;
    const userAvatar = root.querySelector<HTMLImageElement>('[data-user-avatar]')!;
    const userName = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-user-name]')!;
    const logoutBtn = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-logout]')!;
    const form = root.querySelector<HTMLFormElement>('[data-form]')!;
    const replyContext = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-reply-context]')!;
    const replyLabel = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-reply-label]')!;
    const replyCancel = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-reply-cancel]')!;
    const input = root.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>('[data-input]')!;
    const charCount = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-charcount]')!;
    const submitBtn = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-submit]')!;
    const formStatus = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-form-status]')!;
    const toolbar = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-toolbar]')!;
    const sortSelect = root.querySelector<HTMLSelectElement>('[data-sort]')!;
    const statusEl = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-status]')!;
    const listEl = root.querySelector<HTMLOListElement>('[data-list]')!;
    const loadMoreWrap = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-load-more-wrap]')!;
    const loadMoreBtn = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-load-more]')!;
    const tmpl = root.querySelector<HTMLTemplateElement>('[data-item-tmpl]')!;

    if (!SLUG || !API) {
      statusEl.textContent = 'Konfigurasi komentar tidak lengkap.';
      return;
    }

    let currentUser: AuthUser | null = null;
    let allComments: CommentItem[] = [];
    let rootComments: CommentItem[] = [];
    let childrenMap: Record<string, CommentItem[]> = {};
    let visibleRoots = PAGE_SIZE;
    let sortKey: SortKey = 'oldest';
    let turnstileWidgetId: string | null = null;
    let turnstileToken = '';
    let submitting = false;
    let gsiRendered = false;
    let replyTarget: { id: string; name: string } | null = null;
    let cooldownUntil = 0;
    let cooldownTimer: number | null = null;
    let highlightId: string | null = null;

    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(SORT_KEY) as SortKey | null;
      if (saved === 'oldest' || saved === 'newest' || saved === 'popular') {
        sortKey = saved;
      }
    } catch {}

    sortSelect.value = sortKey;

    function readCache(): AuthUser | null {
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem(CACHE_KEY);
        if (!raw) return null;
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<AuthUser> | null;
        if (
          parsed &&
          typeof parsed.sub === 'string' &&
          typeof parsed.name === 'string'
        ) {
          return {
            sub: parsed.sub,
            name: parsed.name,
            email: typeof parsed.email === 'string' ? parsed.email : '',
            picture:
              typeof parsed.picture === 'string' ? parsed.picture : null,
          };
        }
      } catch {}
      return null;
    }

    function loadScript(src: string): Promise<void> {
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const existing = document.querySelector<HTMLScriptElement>(
          `script[src="${src}"]`
        );
        if (existing) {
          if (existing.dataset.loaded === '1') return resolve();
          existing.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(), { once: true });
          existing.addEventListener('error', () => reject(), { once: true });
          return;
        }
        const s = document.createElement('script');
        s.src = src;
        s.async = true;
        s.defer = true;
        s.addEventListener(
          'load',
          () => {
            s.dataset.loaded = '1';
            resolve();
          },
          { once: true }
        );
        s.addEventListener('error', () => reject(), { once: true });
        document.head.appendChild(s);
      });
    }

    function escapeHtml(s: string): string {
      return s
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
        .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
        .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
    }

    function renderMarkdown(text: string): string {
      let s = escapeHtml(text);

      s = s.replace(
        /\|\|([^|\n]+?)\|\|/g,
        '<span class="spoiler" data-spoiler>$1</span>'
      );

      s = s.replace(/`([^`\n]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>');
      s = s.replace(/\*\*([^*\n]+)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
      s = s.replace(/(^|[^*])\*([^*\n]+)\*(?!\*)/g, '$1<em>$2</em>');
      s = s.replace(/~~([^~\n]+)~~/g, '<del>$1</del>');

      s = s.replace(
        /(^|\s)@([A-Za-z0-9_]{2,30})/g,
        '$1<span class="mention">@$2</span>'
      );

      s = s.replace(
        /\[([^\]]+)\]\((https?:\/\/[^\s)]+)\)/g,
        '<a href="$2" target="_blank" rel="nofollow ugc noopener noreferrer">$1</a>'
      );

      s = s.replace(
        /(^|[^"=])(https?:\/\/[^\s<]+)/g,
        (_, prefix, url) =>
          `${prefix}<a href="${url}" target="_blank" rel="nofollow ugc noopener noreferrer">${url}</a>`
      );

      s = s.replace(/\n/g, '<br>');

      return s;
    }

    function formatTime(ts: number): string {
      const d = new Date(ts * 1000);
      const now = Date.now();
      const diff = Math.floor((now - d.getTime()) / 1000);
      if (diff < 60) return 'baru saja';
      if (diff < 3600) return `${Math.floor(diff / 60)} menit lalu`;
      if (diff < 86400) return `${Math.floor(diff / 3600)} jam lalu`;
      if (diff < 604800) return `${Math.floor(diff / 86400)} hari lalu`;
      return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
        day: 'numeric',
        month: 'short',
        year: 'numeric',
      }).format(d);
    }

    function statusBadgeText(status: string): string {
      if (status === 'pending') return 'Menunggu moderasi';
      if (status === 'rejected') return 'Ditolak';
      return '';
    }

    function sortRoots(items: CommentItem[]): CommentItem[] {
      const arr = [...items];
      if (sortKey === 'newest') {
        arr.sort((a, b) => b.createdAt - a.createdAt);
      } else if (sortKey === 'popular') {
        arr.sort((a, b) => {
          if (b.likes !== a.likes) return b.likes - a.likes;
          return b.createdAt - a.createdAt;
        });
      } else {
        arr.sort((a, b) => a.createdAt - b.createdAt);
      }
      return arr;
    }

    function makeItem(item: CommentItem, isReply: boolean): HTMLLIElement {
      const node = tmpl.content.firstElementChild!.cloneNode(
        true
      ) as HTMLLIElement;
      node.dataset.id = item.id;
      node.dataset.parentId = item.parentId ?? '';

      const avatar = node.querySelector<HTMLImageElement>('[data-avatar]')!;
      if (item.user.picture) {
        avatar.src = item.user.picture;
        avatar.alt = item.user.name;
      } else {
        avatar.removeAttribute('src');
        avatar.style.background = 'var(--bg-surface)';
      }

      node.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-name]')!.textContent =
        item.user.name;

      const roleEl = node.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-role]')!;
      if (item.isAdmin) {
        roleEl.hidden = false;
      }

      const timeEl = node.querySelector<HTMLTimeElement>('[data-time]')!;
      timeEl.dateTime = new Date(item.createdAt * 1000).toISOString();
      timeEl.textContent = formatTime(item.createdAt);

      const editedEl = node.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-edited]')!;
      if (item.editedAt) editedEl.hidden = false;

      const bodyEl = node.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-body]')!;
      bodyEl.innerHTML = renderMarkdown(item.body);

      bodyEl.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-spoiler]').forEach((el) => {
        el.addEventListener('click', () => {
          el.classList.toggle('revealed');
        });
      });

      const badge = node.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-badge]')!;
      if (item.isOwn && item.status !== 'approved') {
        const text = statusBadgeText(item.status);
        if (text) {
          badge.textContent = text;
          badge.classList.add(
            item.status === 'rejected' ? 'is-rejected' : 'is-pending'
          );
          badge.hidden = false;
        }
      }

      const likeBtn = node.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
        '[data-action="like"]'
      )!;
      likeBtn.dataset.id = item.id;

      const likeIcon = likeBtn.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-like-icon]')!;
      const likeCount = likeBtn.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-like-count]')!;

      likeIcon.textContent = item.isLiked ? '♥' : '♡';
      if (item.likes > 0) {
        likeCount.textContent = String(item.likes);
        likeCount.hidden = false;
      }
      if (item.isLiked) likeBtn.classList.add('is-liked');
      if (!currentUser) likeBtn.hidden = true;

      const replyBtn = node.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
        '[data-action="reply"]'
      )!;
      if (isReply || !currentUser) {
        replyBtn.hidden = true;
      } else {
        replyBtn.dataset.id = item.id;
        replyBtn.dataset.name = item.user.name;
      }

      const editBtn = node.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
        '[data-action="edit"]'
      )!;
      if (item.canEdit) {
        editBtn.hidden = false;
        editBtn.dataset.id = item.id;
      }

      const deleteBtn = node.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
        '[data-action="delete"]'
      )!;
      if (item.isOwn) {
        deleteBtn.hidden = false;
        deleteBtn.dataset.id = item.id;
        if (item.parentId === null) deleteBtn.dataset.root = '1';
      }

      const reportBtn = node.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
        '[data-action="report"]'
      )!;
      if (currentUser && !item.isOwn) {
        reportBtn.hidden = false;
        reportBtn.dataset.id = item.id;
      }

      const editBox = node.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-edit-box]')!;
      const editInput = node.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>(
        '[data-edit-input]'
      )!;
      editInput.value = item.body;
      editBox.dataset.id = item.id;

      return node;
    }

    function render(): void {
      const visible = rootComments.slice(0, visibleRoots);

      listEl.innerHTML = '';
      for (const r of visible) {
        const node = makeItem(r, false);
        const replies = childrenMap[r.id] ?? [];
        if (replies.length > 0) {
          const repliesEl = node.querySelector<HTMLOListElement>(
            '[data-replies]'
          )!;
          for (const reply of replies) {
            repliesEl.appendChild(makeItem(reply, true));
          }
          repliesEl.hidden = false;
        }
        listEl.appendChild(node);
      }

      countEl.textContent = String(allComments.length);
      listEl.hidden = visible.length === 0;

      const remaining = rootComments.length - visible.length;
      if (remaining > 0) {
        loadMoreWrap.hidden = false;
        loadMoreBtn.textContent = `Muat lebih banyak (${remaining})`;
      } else {
        loadMoreWrap.hidden = true;
      }

      toolbar.hidden = rootComments.length === 0;

      if (highlightId) {
        const target = listEl.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
          `[data-id="${highlightId}"]`
        );
        if (target) {
          target.classList.add('just-posted');
          target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
          window.setTimeout(() => {
            target.classList.remove('just-posted');
          }, 2500);
          highlightId = null;
        }
      }
    }

    async function fetchComments(): Promise<void> {
      statusEl.hidden = false;
      statusEl.textContent = 'Memuat komentar…';
      listEl.hidden = true;
      try {
        const res = await fetch(
          `${API}/api/v1/comments?slug=${encodeURIComponent(SLUG)}`,
          { credentials: 'include' }
        );
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
        const json = (await res.json()) as { data: CommentItem[] };
        allComments = Array.isArray(json.data) ? json.data : [];

        rootComments = allComments.filter((c) => c.parentId === null);
        childrenMap = {};
        for (const c of allComments) {
          if (c.parentId) {
            (childrenMap[c.parentId] ??= []).push(c);
          }
        }

        for (const key of Object.keys(childrenMap)) {
          childrenMap[key]!.sort((a, b) => a.createdAt - b.createdAt);
        }

        rootComments = sortRoots(rootComments);
        visibleRoots = Math.max(PAGE_SIZE, visibleRoots);

        if (allComments.length === 0) {
          statusEl.textContent = 'Belum ada komentar. Jadilah yang pertama.';
          listEl.hidden = true;
          loadMoreWrap.hidden = true;
          toolbar.hidden = true;
          countEl.textContent = '0';
          return;
        }

        render();
        statusEl.hidden = true;
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[comments] fetch failed', err);
        statusEl.textContent = 'Gagal memuat komentar. Coba refresh.';
      }
    }

    function renderGsiButton(): void {
      if (gsiRendered) return;
      if (currentUser) return;
      const g = (window as any).google;
      if (!g?.accounts?.id) return;
      try {
        g.accounts.id.renderButton(gsiSlot, {
          theme: 'outline',
          size: 'large',
          text: 'signin_with',
          shape: 'pill',
        });
        gsiRendered = true;
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[comments] render gsi failed', err);
      }
    }

    function applyUserState(): void {
      skeletonBox.hidden = true;

      if (currentUser) {
        loginBox.hidden = true;
        userBox.hidden = false;
        form.hidden = false;
        userName.textContent = currentUser.name;
        if (currentUser.picture) {
          userAvatar.src = currentUser.picture;
          userAvatar.alt = currentUser.name;
        } else {
          userAvatar.removeAttribute('src');
        }
        mountTurnstile();
      } else {
        loginBox.hidden = false;
        userBox.hidden = true;
        form.hidden = true;
        gsiRendered = false;
        gsiSlot.innerHTML = '';
        renderGsiButton();
      }
    }

    function mountTurnstile(): void {
      if (!SITE_KEY) return;
      if (turnstileWidgetId) return;
      const container = root!.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-turnstile]');
      if (!container) return;
      const ts = (window as any).turnstile;
      if (!ts) return;
      try {
        turnstileWidgetId = ts.render(container, {
          sitekey: SITE_KEY,
          callback: (token: string) => {
            turnstileToken = token;
            updateSubmitState();
          },
          'expired-callback': () => {
            turnstileToken = '';
            updateSubmitState();
          },
          'error-callback': () => {
            turnstileToken = '';
            updateSubmitState();
          },
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[turnstile] render failed', err);
      }
    }

    function isCooldown(): boolean {
      return Date.now() < cooldownUntil;
    }

    function updateSubmitState(): void {
      if (isCooldown()) return;
      const text = input.value.trim();
      const ok =
        !!currentUser && text.length >= 2 && !!turnstileToken && !submitting;
      submitBtn.disabled = !ok;
      submitBtn.textContent = replyTarget ? 'Balas' : 'Kirim';
    }

    function tickCooldown(): void {
      const remain = cooldownUntil - Date.now();
      if (remain <= 0) {
        if (cooldownTimer !== null) {
          window.clearInterval(cooldownTimer);
          cooldownTimer = null;
        }
        cooldownUntil = 0;
        updateSubmitState();
        return;
      }
      submitBtn.disabled = true;
      submitBtn.textContent = `Tunggu ${Math.ceil(remain / 1000)}s`;
    }

    function startCooldown(): void {
      cooldownUntil = Date.now() + COOLDOWN_SEC * 1000;
      if (cooldownTimer !== null) window.clearInterval(cooldownTimer);
      cooldownTimer = window.setInterval(tickCooldown, 500);
      tickCooldown();
    }

    function setFormStatus(
      text: string,
      kind: 'error' | 'success' | null
    ): void {
      formStatus.textContent = text;
      formStatus.classList.remove('is-error', 'is-success');
      if (kind === 'error') formStatus.classList.add('is-error');
      if (kind === 'success') formStatus.classList.add('is-success');
      formStatus.hidden = !text;
    }

    function setReplyTarget(target: { id: string; name: string } | null): void {
      replyTarget = target;
      if (target) {
        replyLabel.textContent = `Membalas ${target.name}`;
        replyContext.hidden = false;
        form.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
        window.setTimeout(() => input.focus(), 300);
      } else {
        replyContext.hidden = true;
        replyLabel.textContent = '';
      }
      updateSubmitState();
    }

    function setCurrentUser(user: AuthUser | null): void {
      const sameUser = (user?.sub ?? null) === (currentUser?.sub ?? null);
      if (sameUser) return;
      currentUser = user;
      applyUserState();
      updateSubmitState();
      fetchComments();
    }

    function onAuthChanged(e: Event): void {
      const detail = (e as CustomEvent<{ user: AuthUser | null }>).detail;
      if (!detail) return;
      setCurrentUser(detail.user);
    }

    document.addEventListener('qimochi:auth-changed', onAuthChanged);
    document.addEventListener('qimochi:gsi-ready', () => renderGsiButton());

    if ((window as any).google?.accounts?.id) {
      renderGsiButton();
    }

    logoutBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      const api = (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu;
      if (api) api.close();
      try {
        await fetch(`${API}/api/v1/auth/logout`, {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'include',
        });
      } catch {}
      setCurrentUser(null);
      turnstileToken = '';
      setFormStatus('', null);
      setReplyTarget(null);
      document.dispatchEvent(
        new CustomEvent('qimochi:auth-changed', { detail: { user: null } })
      );
    });

    replyCancel.addEventListener('click', () => setReplyTarget(null));

    sortSelect.addEventListener('change', () => {
      const v = sortSelect.value as SortKey;
      if (v !== 'oldest' && v !== 'newest' && v !== 'popular') return;
      sortKey = v;
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(SORT_KEY, v);
      } catch {}
      rootComments = sortRoots(rootComments);
      visibleRoots = PAGE_SIZE;
      render();
    });

    loadMoreBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      visibleRoots += PAGE_SIZE;
      render();
    });

    input.addEventListener('input', () => {
      const len = input.value.length;
      charCount.textContent = `${len} / 2000`;
      updateSubmitState();
    });

    listEl.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;

      const likeBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-action="like"]');
      if (likeBtn) {
        const id = likeBtn.dataset.id;
        if (!id || !currentUser) return;
        likeBtn.disabled = true;
        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            `${API}/api/v1/comments/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/like`,
            { method: 'POST', credentials: 'include' }
          );
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
          const json = (await res.json()) as { liked: boolean; likes: number };

          const item = allComments.find((c) => c.id === id);
          if (item) {
            item.isLiked = json.liked;
            item.likes = json.likes;
          }

          const icon = likeBtn.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-like-icon]')!;
          const count = likeBtn.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-like-count]')!;
          icon.textContent = json.liked ? '♥' : '♡';
          if (json.likes > 0) {
            count.textContent = String(json.likes);
            count.hidden = false;
          } else {
            count.hidden = true;
          }
          likeBtn.classList.toggle('is-liked', json.liked);
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[comments] like failed', err);
        } finally {
          likeBtn.disabled = false;
        }
        return;
      }

      const replyBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-action="reply"]');
      if (replyBtn) {
        const id = replyBtn.dataset.id;
        const name = replyBtn.dataset.name;
        if (!id || !name) return;
        setReplyTarget({ id, name });
        return;
      }

      const editBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-action="edit"]');
      if (editBtn) {
        const itemNode = editBtn.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-comment-item]');
        if (!itemNode) return;
        const body = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-body]')!;
        const editBox = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-edit-box]')!;
        const editInput = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>(
          '[data-edit-input]'
        )!;
        const actions = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-actions]')!;
        body.hidden = true;
        actions.hidden = true;
        editBox.hidden = false;
        editInput.focus();
        const len = editInput.value.length;
        editInput.setSelectionRange(len, len);
        return;
      }

      const cancelBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-edit-cancel]');
      if (cancelBtn) {
        const itemNode = cancelBtn.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-comment-item]');
        if (!itemNode) return;
        const body = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-body]')!;
        const editBox = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-edit-box]')!;
        const actions = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-actions]')!;
        const editStatus = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
          '[data-edit-status]'
        )!;
        body.hidden = false;
        actions.hidden = false;
        editBox.hidden = true;
        editStatus.hidden = true;
        return;
      }

      const saveBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-edit-save]');
      if (saveBtn) {
        const itemNode = saveBtn.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-comment-item]');
        if (!itemNode) return;
        const id = itemNode.dataset.id;
        if (!id) return;
        const editInput = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>(
          '[data-edit-input]'
        )!;
        const editStatus = itemNode.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
          '[data-edit-status]'
        )!;
        const text = editInput.value.trim();
        if (text.length < 2) {
          editStatus.textContent = 'Komentar minimal 2 karakter.';
          editStatus.hidden = false;
          return;
        }
        saveBtn.disabled = true;
        editStatus.hidden = true;
        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            `${API}/api/v1/comments/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`,
            {
              method: 'PATCH',
              credentials: 'include',
              headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
              body: JSON.stringify({ body: text }),
            }
          );
          if (!res.ok) {
            const json = (await res.json()) as {
              error?: { code?: string; message: string };
            };
            editStatus.textContent =
              json.error?.message ?? `Gagal (HTTP ${res.status})`;
            editStatus.hidden = false;
            return;
          }
          await fetchComments();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[comments] edit failed', err);
          editStatus.textContent = 'Gagal menyimpan. Coba lagi.';
          editStatus.hidden = false;
        } finally {
          saveBtn.disabled = false;
        }
        return;
      }

      const deleteBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>(
        '[data-action="delete"]'
      );
      if (deleteBtn) {
        const id = deleteBtn.dataset.id;
        if (!id) return;
        const isRoot = deleteBtn.dataset.root === '1';
        const msg = isRoot
          ? 'Hapus komentar ini beserta semua balasannya?'
          : 'Hapus komentar ini?';
        if (!window.confirm(msg)) return;

        deleteBtn.disabled = true;
        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            `${API}/api/v1/comments/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`,
            { method: 'DELETE', credentials: 'include' }
          );
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
          await fetchComments();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[comments] delete failed', err);
          window.alert('Gagal menghapus komentar.');
          deleteBtn.disabled = false;
        }
        return;
      }

      const reportBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>(
        '[data-action="report"]'
      );
      if (reportBtn) {
        const id = reportBtn.dataset.id;
        if (!id) return;
        const reason = window.prompt(
          'Alasan melaporkan komentar ini? (spam, kasar, spoiler, dll)'
        );
        if (reason === null) return;
        reportBtn.disabled = true;
        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            `${API}/api/v1/comments/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/report`,
            {
              method: 'POST',
              credentials: 'include',
              headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
              body: JSON.stringify({ reason: reason.trim() }),
            }
          );
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
          window.alert('Laporan terkirim. Terima kasih.');
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[comments] report failed', err);
          window.alert('Gagal mengirim laporan.');
        } finally {
          reportBtn.disabled = false;
        }
        return;
      }
    });

    form.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (submitting || isCooldown()) return;

      const text = input.value.trim();
      if (text.length < 2) {
        setFormStatus('Komentar minimal 2 karakter.', 'error');
        return;
      }
      if (!turnstileToken) {
        setFormStatus('Selesaikan captcha dulu.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      submitting = true;
      updateSubmitState();
      setFormStatus('Mengirim…', null);

      try {
        const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/v1/comments`, {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'include',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            slug: SLUG,
            body: text,
            parentId: replyTarget?.id ?? null,
            turnstileToken,
          }),
        });

        const json = (await res.json()) as {
          ok?: boolean;
          status?: string;
          reason?: string;
          data?: { id: string };
          error?: { code?: string; message: string };
        };

        if (!res.ok) {
          if (json.error?.code === 'COMMENT_REJECTED') {
            turnstileToken = '';
            if (turnstileWidgetId) {
              try {
                (window as any).turnstile.reset(turnstileWidgetId);
              } catch {}
            }
            setFormStatus(json.error.message, 'error');
            input.focus();
            updateSubmitState();
            return;
          }

          const msg = json.error?.message ?? `Gagal (HTTP ${res.status})`;
          setFormStatus(msg, 'error');
          return;
        }

        const newId = json.data?.id ?? null;
        input.value = '';
        charCount.textContent = '0 / 2000';
        turnstileToken = '';
        if (turnstileWidgetId) {
          try {
            (window as any).turnstile.reset(turnstileWidgetId);
          } catch {}
        }
        setReplyTarget(null);

        const newStatus = json.status ?? 'pending';

        if (newStatus === 'approved') {
          setFormStatus('Komentar kamu langsung tampil.', 'success');
        } else {
          setFormStatus('Komentar terkirim. Menunggu moderasi.', 'success');
        }
        startCooldown();
        if (newId) highlightId = newId;
        await fetchComments();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[comments] submit failed', err);
        setFormStatus('Gagal mengirim. Coba lagi.', 'error');
      } finally {
        submitting = false;
        if (!isCooldown()) updateSubmitState();
      }
    });

    (function bootstrap(): void {
      currentUser = readCache();
      applyUserState();
      updateSubmitState();
    })();

    (async () => {
      await Promise.allSettled([
        fetchComments(),
        loadScript(
          'https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/api.js?render=explicit'
        )
          .then(() => {
            if (currentUser) mountTurnstile();
          })
          .catch((err) =>
            console.error('[comments] turnstile load failed', err)
          ),
      ]);

      const menu = (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu;
      if (menu) {
        const u = menu.getUser();
        if ((u?.sub ?? null) !== (currentUser?.sub ?? null)) {
          setCurrentUser(u ?? null);
        }
      }

      renderGsiButton();
      updateSubmitState();
    })();
  })();
</script>

===== FILE: src/components/Head.astro =====
---
import { SITE } from '~/constants';
import { Font } from "astro:assets";

interface Props {
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  image?: string;
  canonical?: string;
  noindex?: boolean;
  type?: 'website' | 'article' | 'video.other';
  publishedTime?: string;
}

const {
  title,
  description = SITE.description,
  image,
  canonical,
  noindex = false,
  type = 'website',
  publishedTime,
} = Astro.props;

const canonicalURL = canonical
  ? new URL(canonical, Astro.site)
  : new URL(Astro.url.pathname, Astro.site);

const fullTitle = title === SITE.name ? title : `${title} | ${SITE.name}`;

const ogImage = image
  ? new URL(image, Astro.site)
  : SITE.ogImage
    ? new URL(SITE.ogImage, Astro.site)
    : null;

const twitterCard = ogImage ? 'summary_large_image' : 'summary';

const GA_ID = import.meta.env.PUBLIC_GA_ID;
---

<meta charset="UTF-8" />
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, viewport-fit=cover" />

<title>{fullTitle}</title>
<meta name="description" content={description} />
<link rel="canonical" href={canonicalURL} />

{noindex ? (
  <meta name="robots" content="noindex, follow" />
) : (
  <meta name="robots" content="index, follow" />
)}

<meta property="og:type" content={type} />
<meta property="og:title" content={fullTitle} />
<meta property="og:description" content={description} />
<meta property="og:url" content={canonicalURL} />
{ogImage && <meta property="og:image" content={ogImage} />}
<meta property="og:site_name" content={SITE.name} />
<meta property="og:locale" content={SITE.locale} />
{publishedTime && (
  <meta property="article:published_time" content={publishedTime} />
)}

<meta name="twitter:card" content={twitterCard} />
<meta name="twitter:title" content={fullTitle} />
<meta name="twitter:description" content={description} />
{ogImage && <meta name="twitter:image" content={ogImage} />}

<meta name="theme-color" content="#0a0a0f" />
<meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-title" content={SITE.name} />

<link rel="icon" type="image/png" href="/favicon-32.png" sizes="32x32" />
<link rel="icon" type="image/svg+xml" href="/favicon.svg" />
<link rel="shortcut icon" href="/favicon.ico" />
<link rel="apple-touch-icon" sizes="180x180" href="/apple-touch-icon.png" />
<link rel="manifest" href="/site.webmanifest" />

<link rel="sitemap" href="/sitemap-index.xml" />
<link
  rel="alternate"
  type="application/rss+xml"
  title={`${SITE.name} — Blog`}
  href="/rss.xml"
/>

import { SITE, NAV_LINKS } from '~/constants';
import { Image } from 'astro:assets';
import logoImg from '~/assets/images/logo/logo.svg';
import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';
import MobileMenu from './MobileMenu.astro';

const currentPath = Astro.url.pathname;

function isActive(href: string): boolean {
  if (href === '/') return currentPath === '/';
  const matches = currentPath === href || currentPath.startsWith(href);
  if (!matches) return false;
  const hasMoreSpecific = NAV_LINKS.some(({ href: otherHref }) => {
    if (otherHref === href) return false;
    if (otherHref === '/') return false;
    if (otherHref.length <= href.length) return false;
    return currentPath === otherHref || currentPath.startsWith(otherHref);
  });
  return !hasMoreSpecific;
}
---

          width={64}

<script>
  const menu = document.querySelector<HTMLDetailsElement>('.menu');

  if (menu) {
    const mobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 50rem)');

    mobile.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      if (e.matches && menu.hasAttribute('open')) {
        menu.removeAttribute('open');
      }
    });

    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (!mobile.matches) return;
      if (!menu.hasAttribute('open')) return;
      if (!menu.contains(e.target as Node)) menu.removeAttribute('open');
    });

    menu.querySelectorAll('.nav a').forEach((a) => {
      a.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (mobile.matches) menu.removeAttribute('open');
      });
    });

    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && mobile.matches && menu.hasAttribute('open')) {
        menu.removeAttribute('open');
      }
    });
  }
</script>

===== FILE: src/components/NovelCard.astro =====

import Badge from '~/user-components/Badge.astro';
import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';
import { URLS, STATUS_VARIANT, type NovelStatus } from '~/constants';

interface CardNovel {
  id: string;
  data: {
    title: string;
    cover: string;
    status: string;
    author: string;
    synopsis?: string;
    chapters?: { number: number }[];
  };
}

interface Props {
  mode?: 'grid' | 'template';
  novel?: CardNovel;
  rich?: boolean;
}

const { mode = 'grid', novel, rich = true } = Astro.props;
const isTemplate = mode === 'template';

if (!isTemplate && !novel) {
  throw new Error('[NovelCard] Prop "novel" wajib diisi.');
}

const id = novel?.id ?? '';
const data = novel?.data;

const variant = data
  ? STATUS_VARIANT[data.status as NovelStatus] ?? 'default'
  : 'default';

const chapters = data?.chapters ?? [];
const totalChapters = chapters.length;
const firstChapter = totalChapters > 0
  ? Math.min(...chapters.map((c) => c.number))
  : 1;

const chapterLabel = totalChapters > 0 ? `${totalChapters} Chapter` : null;
const authorLabel = data?.author && data.author !== 'Unknown' ? data.author : null;

function plainExcerpt(input: string | undefined, maxLen = 110): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  const cleaned = input
    .replace(/!\[[^\]]*\]\([^)]*\)/g, '')
    .replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\([^)]*\)/g, '$1')
    .replace(/[*_`>#]/g, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
  return cleaned.length <= maxLen
    ? cleaned
    : cleaned.slice(0, maxLen).trimEnd() + '…';
}

const excerpt = plainExcerpt(data?.synopsis);
---

<script>
  (function () {
    function setupCoverImage(img: HTMLImageElement): void {
      if (img.dataset.ready === '1') return;
      img.dataset.ready = '1';

      const wrap = img.parentElement;
      const placeholder = wrap?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.cover-placeholder') ?? null;

      const stopShimmer = () => placeholder?.classList.remove('is-loading');
      const fail = () => {
        img.hidden = true;
        stopShimmer();
      };

      if (img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
        stopShimmer();
        return;
      }
      if (img.complete && img.naturalWidth === 0) {
        fail();
        return;
      }

      img.addEventListener('load', stopShimmer, { once: true });
      img.addEventListener('error', fail, { once: true });
    }

    function init(scope: ParentNode = document): void {
      scope.querySelectorAll<HTMLImageElement>('[data-cover-img]').forEach(setupCoverImage);
    }

    init();
    window.addEventListener('qimochi:covers', (e) => {
      const detail = (e as CustomEvent<ParentNode>).detail;
      if (detail) init(detail);
    });
  })();
</script>

  chapterNumber,
  novelTitle,
  chapterHeading,
  tocUrl,
  prevUrl,
  nextUrl,
} = Astro.props;
---

<!-- Terapkan pengaturan sebelum konten dirender (anti flash tema). -->
<script is:inline>
  (function () {
    try {
      var s = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('yukionovel:reader') || '{}') || {};
      var r = document.documentElement;
      if (s.theme === 'light' || s.theme === 'sepia') r.dataset.readerTheme = s.theme;
      if (s.font === 'sans') r.dataset.readerFont = 'sans';
      if (typeof s.size === 'number' && s.size >= 14 && s.size <= 28) {
        r.style.setProperty('--reader-size', s.size / 16 + 'rem');
      }
      if (s.spacing === 1.6 || s.spacing === 1.8 || s.spacing === 2) {
        r.style.setProperty('--reader-lh', String(s.spacing));
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  })();
</script>

{nextUrl && <link rel="prefetch" href={nextUrl} />}

<div
  class="reader-wrapper"
  data-reader
  data-chrome="show"
  data-slug={seriesSlug}

  data-prev={prevUrl}
  data-next={nextUrl}
>
  <header class="reader-header">
    <a href={tocUrl} class="reader-btn" aria-label="Kembali ke daftar chapter">
      <Icon name="arrow-left" size={20} />
    </a>

    <div class="reader-header__title">
      <span class="reader-header__novel">{novelTitle}</span>
      <span class="reader-header__chapter">{chapterHeading}</span>
    </div>

    <button
      type="button"
      class="reader-btn reader-btn--text"
      data-settings-toggle
      aria-expanded="false"
      aria-controls="reader-panel"
      aria-label="Pengaturan bacaan"
    >
      Aa
    </button>

    <div class="reader-progress" data-progress aria-hidden="true"></div>
  </header>

  <div class="reader-panel" id="reader-panel" data-panel hidden>
    <div class="reader-panel__row">
      <span class="reader-panel__label">Tema</span>
      <div class="seg" role="group" aria-label="Tema">
        <button type="button" data-set="theme" data-value="dark" aria-pressed="false">Gelap</button>
        <button type="button" data-set="theme" data-value="sepia" aria-pressed="false">Sepia</button>
        <button type="button" data-set="theme" data-value="light" aria-pressed="false">Terang</button>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="reader-panel__row">
      <span class="reader-panel__label">Font</span>
      <div class="seg" role="group" aria-label="Jenis font">
        <button type="button" data-set="font" data-value="serif" aria-pressed="false">Serif</button>
        <button type="button" data-set="font" data-value="sans" aria-pressed="false">Sans</button>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="reader-panel__row">
      <span class="reader-panel__label">Ukuran</span>
      <div class="seg seg--size" role="group" aria-label="Ukuran teks">
        <button type="button" data-size-step="-1" aria-label="Perkecil teks">A−</button>
        <output data-size-label aria-live="polite">18</output>
        <button type="button" data-size-step="1" aria-label="Perbesar teks">A+</button>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="reader-panel__row">
      <span class="reader-panel__label">Jarak</span>
      <div class="seg" role="group" aria-label="Jarak baris">
        <button type="button" data-set="spacing" data-value="1.6" aria-pressed="false">Rapat</button>
        <button type="button" data-set="spacing" data-value="1.8" aria-pressed="false">Normal</button>
        <button type="button" data-set="spacing" data-value="2" aria-pressed="false">Lega</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <article class="reader-content">
    <div class="reader-content__inner">
      <a href={tocUrl} class="reader-novel-link">{novelTitle}</a>
      <h1 class="reader-chapter-title">{chapterHeading}</h1>

      <div class="reader-text markdown">
        <slot />
      </div>

      <nav class="reader-end-nav" aria-label="Navigasi chapter">
        {prevUrl ? (
          <a href={prevUrl} class="reader-end-btn" rel="prev">
            <Icon name="arrow-left" size={16} /> Prev
          </a>
        ) : (
          <span class="reader-end-btn is-disabled" aria-hidden="true"></span>
        )}

        {nextUrl ? (
          <a href={nextUrl} class="reader-end-btn reader-end-btn--next" rel="next">
            Next <Icon name="arrow-right" size={16} />
          </a>
        ) : (
          <span class="reader-end-btn is-disabled" aria-hidden="true"></span>
        )}
      </nav>

      {!nextUrl && (
        <p class="reader-latest">
          Kamu sudah sampai chapter terbaru. <a href={tocUrl}>Kembali ke daftar chapter</a>
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  </article>

  <nav class="reader-bottom-bar" aria-label="Navigasi chapter">
    <a href={tocUrl} class="reader-bar-btn">
      <Icon name="list" size={20} />
      <span>Daftar</span>
    </a>

    {prevUrl ? (
      <a href={prevUrl} class="reader-bar-btn" rel="prev">
        <Icon name="arrow-left" size={20} />
        <span>Prev</span>
      </a>
    ) : (
      <span class="reader-bar-btn is-disabled" aria-hidden="true"></span>
    )}

    {nextUrl ? (

        <span>Next</span>

  </nav>

<script>
  type Theme = 'dark' | 'sepia' | 'light';
  type Font = 'serif' | 'sans';

  interface ReaderSettings {
    theme: Theme;
    font: Font;
    size: number;
    spacing: number;
  }

  const SETTINGS_KEY = 'yukionovel:reader';
  const SIZE_MIN = 14;
  const SIZE_MAX = 28;
  const SPACINGS = [1.6, 1.8, 2];
  const DEFAULTS: ReaderSettings = { theme: 'dark', font: 'serif', size: 18, spacing: 1.8 };

  function clamp(n: number, min: number, max: number): number {
    return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, n));
  }

  function loadSettings(): ReaderSettings {
    try {
      const raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SETTINGS_KEY) ?? '{}') as Partial<ReaderSettings> | null;
      if (!raw) return { ...DEFAULTS };
      return {
        theme: raw.theme === 'light' || raw.theme === 'sepia' ? raw.theme : 'dark',
        font: raw.font === 'sans' ? 'sans' : 'serif',
        size: typeof raw.size === 'number' ? clamp(Math.round(raw.size), SIZE_MIN, SIZE_MAX) : DEFAULTS.size,
        spacing: typeof raw.spacing === 'number' && SPACINGS.includes(raw.spacing) ? raw.spacing : DEFAULTS.spacing,
      };
    } catch {
      return { ...DEFAULTS };
    }
  }

  function saveSettings(s: ReaderSettings): void {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(SETTINGS_KEY, JSON.stringify(s));
    } catch {}
  }

  function initReader(root: HTMLElement): void {
    const slug = root.dataset.slug ?? '';
    const chapter = Number(root.dataset.chapter);
    const prevUrl = root.dataset.prev;
    const nextUrl = root.dataset.next;
    const lastKey = `yukionovel:last:${slug}`;

    const html = document.documentElement;
    const progress = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-progress]');
    const panel = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-panel]');
    const toggle = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-settings-toggle]');
    const sizeLabel = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-size-label]');

    const settings = loadSettings();

    function applySettings(): void {
      if (settings.theme === 'dark') delete html.dataset.readerTheme;
      else html.dataset.readerTheme = settings.theme;

      if (settings.font === 'sans') html.dataset.readerFont = 'sans';
      else delete html.dataset.readerFont;

      html.style.setProperty('--reader-size', `${settings.size / 16}rem`);
      html.style.setProperty('--reader-lh', String(settings.spacing));

      if (sizeLabel) sizeLabel.textContent = String(settings.size);

      root.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-set]').forEach((btn) => {
        const key = btn.dataset.set as keyof ReaderSettings | undefined;
        if (!key) return;
        btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(String(settings[key]) === btn.dataset.value));
      });
    }

    function commit(): void {
      applySettings();
      saveSettings(settings);
    }

    applySettings();

    /* ---------- Panel ---------- */
    function setPanel(open: boolean): void {
      if (!panel || !toggle) return;
      panel.hidden = !open;
      toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
      if (open) root.dataset.chrome = 'show';
    }

    toggle?.addEventListener('click', () => setPanel(panel?.hidden ?? false));

    panel?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;

      const setBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-set]');
      if (setBtn) {
        const key = setBtn.dataset.set;
        const value = setBtn.dataset.value ?? '';

        if (key === 'theme' && (value === 'dark' || value === 'sepia' || value === 'light')) {
          settings.theme = value;
        } else if (key === 'font' && (value === 'serif' || value === 'sans')) {
          settings.font = value;
        } else if (key === 'spacing') {
          const n = Number(value);
          if (SPACINGS.includes(n)) settings.spacing = n;
        }
        commit();
        return;
      }

      const stepBtn = target.closest<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-size-step]');
      if (stepBtn) {
        const step = Number(stepBtn.dataset.sizeStep);
        if (Number.isFinite(step)) {
          settings.size = clamp(settings.size + step, SIZE_MIN, SIZE_MAX);
          commit();
        }
      }
    });

    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (!panel || panel.hidden) return;
      const target = e.target as Node;
      if (panel.contains(target) || toggle?.contains(target)) return;
      setPanel(false);
    });

    /* ---------- Last read ---------- */
    function readLast(): { n: number; r: number } | null {
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem(lastKey);
        if (!raw) return null;
        const p = JSON.parse(raw) as { n?: unknown; r?: unknown } | null;
        if (p && typeof p.n === 'number' && typeof p.r === 'number') {
          return { n: p.n, r: p.r };
        }
      } catch {}
      return null;
    }

    function writeLast(r: number): void {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(lastKey, JSON.stringify({ n: chapter, r: Number(r.toFixed(4)) }));
      } catch {}
    }

    const saved = readLast();
    if (!saved || saved.n !== chapter) writeLast(0);

    function restorePosition(): void {
      if (!saved || saved.n !== chapter) return;
      if (location.hash || window.scrollY > 50) return;
      if (saved.r < 0.02 || saved.r > 0.97) return;
      const max = document.documentElement.scrollHeight - window.innerHeight;
      if (max > 0) window.scrollTo(0, max * saved.r);
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'complete') restorePosition();
    else window.addEventListener('load', restorePosition, { once: true });

    /* ---------- Scroll: progress, hide chrome, save position ---------- */
    let lastY = window.scrollY;
    let ticking = false;
    let saveTimer: number | null = null;

    function onScroll(): void {
      if (ticking) return;
      ticking = true;

      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        ticking = false;

        const y = window.scrollY;
        const max = document.documentElement.scrollHeight - window.innerHeight;
        const ratio = max > 0 ? clamp(y / max, 0, 1) : 1;

        if (progress) progress.style.transform = `scaleX(${ratio})`;

        const delta = y - lastY;
        const panelOpen = panel ? !panel.hidden : false;

        if (y < 80 || ratio > 0.98 || delta < -6 || panelOpen) {
          root.dataset.chrome = 'show';
        } else if (delta > 6) {
          root.dataset.chrome = 'hide';
        }
        lastY = y;

        if (saveTimer !== null) window.clearTimeout(saveTimer);
        saveTimer = window.setTimeout(() => writeLast(ratio >= 0.98 ? 0 : ratio), 400);
      });
    }

    window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
    onScroll();

    /* ---------- Keyboard ---------- */
    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.defaultPrevented || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.altKey || e.shiftKey) return;

      const t = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
      if (t && (t.isContentEditable || /^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(t.tagName))) return;

      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        setPanel(false);
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' && prevUrl) {
        location.assign(prevUrl);
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' && nextUrl) {
        location.assign(nextUrl);
      }
    });
  }

  const readerRoot = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-reader]');
  if (readerRoot) initReader(readerRoot);
</script>

===== FILE: src/components/Pagination.astro =====
---
import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';

interface Props {
  current: number;
  total: number;
  base: string;
}

const { current, total, base } = Astro.props;

if (total <= 1) return null;

function urlFor(page: number): string {
  return page === 1 ? `${base}/` : `${base}/${page}/`;
}

function buildPages(): (number | '...')[] {
  if (total <= 7) {
    return Array.from({ length: total }, (_, i) => i + 1);
  }

  const pages: (number | '...')[] = [1];

  if (current > 3) pages.push('...');

  const start = Math.max(2, current - 1);
  const end = Math.min(total - 1, current + 1);

  for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) pages.push(i);

  if (current < total - 2) pages.push('...');
  pages.push(total);

  return pages;
}

const pages = buildPages();
---

<nav class="pagination" aria-label="Navigasi halaman">
  {current > 1 ? (
    <a href={urlFor(current - 1)} class="page-btn" rel="prev" aria-label="Halaman sebelumnya">
      <Icon name="arrow-left" />
    </a>
  ) : (
    <span class="page-btn disabled" aria-hidden="true">
      <Icon name="arrow-left" />
    </span>
  )}

  {pages.map(p =>
    p === '...' ? (
      <span class="page-ellipsis">…</span>
    ) : p === current ? (
      <span class="page-num active" aria-current="page">{p}</span>
    ) : (
      <a href={urlFor(p)} class="page-num">{p}</a>
    )
  )}

  {current < total ? (
    <a href={urlFor(current + 1)} class="page-btn" rel="next" aria-label="Halaman berikutnya">
      <Icon name="arrow-right" />
    </a>
  ) : (
    <span class="page-btn disabled" aria-hidden="true">
      <Icon name="arrow-right" />
    </span>
  )}
</nav>

<script>
  (function () {
    interface AuthUser {
      sub: string;
      name: string;
      email: string;
      picture: string | null;
    }

    function getUser(): AuthUser | null {
      try {
        const menu = (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu;
        if (menu?.getUser) return menu.getUser();
      } catch {}
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem('qimochi:user-cache');
        if (!raw) return null;
        return JSON.parse(raw);
      } catch {
        return null;
      }
    }

    function openUserMenu(): void {
      const menu = (window as any).__qimochiUserMenu;
      if (menu?.open) menu.open();
    }

    function initDialog(btn: HTMLButtonElement): void {
      if (btn.dataset.bound === '1') return;
      btn.dataset.bound = '1';

      const dialogId = btn.dataset.dialogId ?? '';
      if (!dialogId) return;

      const dialog = document.getElementById(dialogId) as HTMLDialogElement | null;
      if (!dialog) return;

      const form = dialog.querySelector<HTMLFormElement>('[data-report-form]');
      const closeBtn = dialog.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-report-close]');
      const cancelBtn = dialog.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-report-cancel]');
      const statusEl = dialog.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-report-status]');
      const slugEl = dialog.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-report-slug]');
      const episodeEl = dialog.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-report-episode]');

      const slug = btn.dataset.slug ?? '';
      const episode = btn.dataset.episode ?? '';
      const server = btn.dataset.server ?? '';
      const quality = btn.dataset.quality ?? '';
      const api = (btn.dataset.api ?? '').replace(/\/$/, '');

      if (slugEl) slugEl.textContent = slug.replace(/-/g, ' ');
      if (episodeEl) episodeEl.textContent = episode;

      function openDialog(): void {
        if (!getUser()) {
          openUserMenu();
          return;
        }
        dialog.showModal();
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      }

      function closeDialog(): void {
        if (dialog.open) dialog.close();
      }

      dialog.addEventListener('close', () => {
        document.body.style.overflow = '';
        if (statusEl) {
          statusEl.hidden = true;
          statusEl.classList.remove('is-error', 'is-success');
          statusEl.textContent = '';
        }
        form?.reset();
      });

      btn.addEventListener('click', openDialog);
      closeBtn?.addEventListener('click', closeDialog);
      cancelBtn?.addEventListener('click', closeDialog);

      dialog.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        if (e.target === dialog) closeDialog();
      });

      form?.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (statusEl) {
          statusEl.hidden = true;
          statusEl.classList.remove('is-error', 'is-success');
        }

        const submitBtn = form.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
          'button[type="submit"]'
        );
        if (submitBtn) submitBtn.disabled = true;

        const fd = new FormData(form);
        const reason = String(fd.get('reason') ?? '');
        const note = String(fd.get('note') ?? '').trim();

        try {
          const res = await fetch(`${api}/api/v1/reports`, {
            method: 'POST',
            credentials: 'include',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              slug,
              episode: Number(episode),
              server,
              quality,
              reason,
              note,
            }),
          });

          const json = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));

          if (res.status === 401) {
            closeDialog();
            openUserMenu();
            return;
          }

          if (res.status === 429) {
            if (statusEl) {
              statusEl.textContent =
                json?.error?.message ?? 'Terlalu banyak laporan. Coba lagi nanti.';
              statusEl.classList.add('is-error');
              statusEl.hidden = false;
            }
            return;
          }

          if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);

          if (statusEl) {
            statusEl.textContent = 'Laporan terkirim. Terima kasih!';
            statusEl.classList.add('is-success');
            statusEl.hidden = false;
          }

          window.setTimeout(() => closeDialog(), 1500);
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[report] submit failed', err);
          if (statusEl) {
            statusEl.textContent = 'Gagal mengirim. Coba lagi.';
            statusEl.classList.add('is-error');
            statusEl.hidden = false;
          }
        } finally {
          if (submitBtn) submitBtn.disabled = false;
        }
      });
    }

    function initAll(scope: ParentNode = document): void {
      scope
        .querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-report-btn]')
        .forEach(initDialog);
    }

    initAll();
    document.addEventListener('astro:page-load', () => initAll());
  })();
</script>

===== FILE: src/components/RssFeed.astro =====

import LinkButton from '~/user-components/LinkButton.astro';
import SectionLabel from '~/components/SectionLabel.astro';

interface Props {
  label?: string;
  title?: string;
  description?: string;
  href?: string;

import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';

interface Props {
  title: string;
  url?: string;
  class?: string;
}

const { title, url, class: className } = Astro.props;

const shareUrl = url ?? Astro.url.href;
---

<div class:list={['share-wrap', className]}>
  <button
    type="button"
    class="icon-btn"
    data-share-btn
    data-title={title}
    data-url={shareUrl}
    aria-label="Bagikan"
    title="Bagikan"
  >
    <Icon name="share" size={20} />
  </button>

  <span
    class="share-toast"
    data-share-toast
    role="status"
    aria-live="polite"
    hidden
  >
    Link disalin
  </span>
</div>

<script>
  (function () {
    function init(btn: HTMLButtonElement): void {
      if (btn.dataset.bound === '1') return;
      btn.dataset.bound = '1';

      const wrap = btn.closest<HTMLElement>('.share-wrap');
      const toast = wrap?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-share-toast]') ?? null;
      let toastTimer: number | null = null;

      function showToast(): void {
        if (!toast) return;
        toast.hidden = false;
        if (toastTimer !== null) window.clearTimeout(toastTimer);
        toastTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
          toast.hidden = true;
          toastTimer = null;
        }, 2000);
      }

      btn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const t = btn.dataset.title ?? document.title;
        const u = btn.dataset.url ?? location.href;

        if (typeof navigator.share === 'function') {
          try {
            await navigator.share({ title: t, url: u });
            return;
          } catch {}
        }

        try {
          await navigator.clipboard.writeText(u);
          showToast();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[share] copy failed', err);
        }
      });
    }

    document
      .querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-share-btn]')
      .forEach(init);
  })();
</script>

<style>
  .share-wrap {

    display: inline-flex;

===== FILE: src/content.config.ts =====
import { defineCollection } from 'astro:content';
import { z } from 'astro/zod';
import { glob } from 'astro/loaders';

const Slug = z.string().regex(/^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/);

const series = defineCollection({
  loader: glob({ pattern: '*/index.md', base: './src/content/novel' }),
  schema: z.object({
    title: z.string(),
    author: z.string().default('Unknown'),
    cover: z.string().default(''),
    synopsis: z.string().default(''),
    status: z.enum(['Ongoing', 'Completed', 'Hiatus', 'Dropped']).default('Ongoing'),
    genres: z.array(Slug).default([]),
    year: z.number().int().min(1900).max(2100).optional(),
    draft: z.boolean().default(false),
  }),
});

const chapters = defineCollection({
  loader: glob({ pattern: '*/[0-9]*.md', base: './src/content/novel' }),
  schema: z.object({
    chapter: z.number().int().positive(),
    chapterTitle: z.string().optional(),
    publishedAt: z.coerce.date(),
    draft: z.boolean().default(false),
  }),
});

const blog = defineCollection({
  loader: glob({ pattern: '**/*.md', base: './src/content/blog' }),
  schema: z.object({
    title: z.string().max(120),
    description: z.string().max(200),
    cover: z.string().optional(),
    date: z.coerce.date(),
    updatedAt: z.coerce.date().optional(),
    author: z.string().default('Admin'),
    category: z.enum(['News', 'Review', 'List', 'Guide', 'Update']),
    tags: z.array(z.string()).default([]),
    draft: z.boolean().default(false),
  }),
});

export const collections = { series, chapters, blog };
===== FILE: src/content/blog/selamat-datang-di-qimochi.md =====
---
title: "Selamat Datang di Qimochi — Nonton Anime Sub Indo, Ringan & Cepat"
description: "Perkenalan lengkap tentang Qimochi — fitur, cara pakai, dan komitmen kami untuk pengalaman nonton anime yang ringan dan cepat."
cover: /og.jpg
date: 2026-02-15
updatedAt: 2026-10-16
author: Admin
category: Update
tags: [Announcement, Qimochi, Guide]
draft: false
---

Selamat datang di **Qimochi** — tempat nonton anime subtitle Indonesia
dengan pengalaman yang ringan, cepat, dan tanpa ribet.

## Apa Itu Qimochi?

Qimochi adalah koleksi anime yang bisa kamu tonton langsung atau download
dalam berbagai kualitas. Kami fokus pada tiga hal utama:

### 1. Kecepatan

Situs dibangun dengan teknologi statis modern. Setiap halaman dimuat dalam
waktu kurang dari 1 detik, bahkan di koneksi lambat.

### 2. Kemudahan

Navigasi simpel — tanpa popup, tanpa iklan mengganggu, tanpa redirect aneh.
Kamu datang untuk nonton, kami pastikan itu yang kamu dapat.

### 3. Kelengkapan

Koleksi anime terus bertambah. Dari judul klasik sampai musim terbaru,
semuanya ada di satu tempat.

## Fitur Utama

- **Streaming langsung** — tonton tanpa download
- **Download** — pilih kualitas, unduh per episode
- **Multi-kualitas** — 360p, 480p, 720p, 1080p
- **Multi-server** — ganti server kalau yang satu lag
- **Pencarian canggih** — cari dengan typo tetap ketemu
- **Archive lengkap** — jelajahi berdasarkan abjad
- **Genre** — temukan sesuai selera
- **Daftar Saya** — simpan anime favorit (login Google)
- **Riwayat tonton** — lanjut dari episode terakhir
- **Rating komunitas** — beri nilai dan lihat rekomendasi
- **Komentar & reaksi** — diskusi bareng penggemar lain

## Cara Menggunakan

### 1. Jelajahi Anime

Buka halaman [Anime](/anime/) untuk daftar lengkap, atau langsung ke:

- [Ongoing](/anime/ongoing/) — anime yang sedang tayang
- [Completed](/anime/completed/) — anime yang sudah tamat
- [Genre](/anime/genre/) — jelajahi berdasarkan genre

### 2. Cari Anime

Halaman [Cari](/search/) mendukung pencarian dengan toleransi typo.
Ketik judul dengan salah huruf pun tetap ketemu.

### 3. Pilih Episode

Buka halaman detail anime untuk daftar episode lengkap. Klik episode
yang mau ditonton.

### 4. Pilih Server & Kualitas

Di halaman tonton, kamu bisa memilih:

- **Kualitas** — dari 360p (hemat kuota) sampai 1080p (kualitas terbaik)
- **Server** — ganti-ganti kalau yang satu bermasalah

Pilihanmu akan **diingat otomatis** untuk kunjungan berikutnya.

### 5. Simpan & Beri Rating

**Login dengan Google** untuk:

- **Bookmark** anime favoritmu
- **Catat riwayat tonton** — lanjut nonton dari episode terakhir
- **Beri rating** — bantu pengguna lain temukan anime bagus
- **Komentar** — diskusi bareng komunitas

## Yang Perlu Diketahui

Kami **tidak menyimpan file video** di server kami. Semua konten di-embed
dari layanan pihak ketiga yang tersedia bebas di internet. Kami hanya
menyediakan tautan untuk keperluan referensi.

Untuk informasi lebih lanjut, lihat halaman [DMCA](/dmca/) dan
[Disclaimer](/disclaimer/).

## Tetap Update

Jangan lewatkan anime baru:

- **RSS Feed** — subscribe di [RSS](/rss.xml) untuk update artikel baru
- **Blog** — pantau [Blog](/blog/) untuk review dan berita

## Apa Selanjutnya?

Kami terus mengembangkan Qimochi. Beberapa fitur yang sedang dalam
perencanaan:

- Notifikasi episode baru
- Rekomendasi personal berdasarkan rating kamu
- Integrasi komunitas Discord
- Peningkatan pencarian dan filter

Pantau terus blog ini untuk update terbaru. Selamat menjelajah! 🎉
