===== FILE: src/lib/blog.ts =====
import { getCollection } from 'astro:content';
import type { CollectionEntry } from 'astro:content';

export type BlogEntry = CollectionEntry<'blog'>;

/**
 * Ambil semua artikel blog yang published (bukan draft).
 * Sort by date descending (terbaru dulu).
 */
export async function getPublishedPosts(): Promise<BlogEntry[]> {
  const allPosts = await getCollection('blog', ({ data }) => !data.draft);
  return [...allPosts].sort(
    (a, b) => b.data.date.getTime() - a.data.date.getTime()
  );
}

/**
 * Ambil artikel by slug — return null kalau draft atau tidak ada.
 */
export async function getPublishedPostBySlug(
  slug: string
): Promise<BlogEntry | null> {
  const allPosts = await getCollection('blog');
  const post = allPosts.find(p => p.id === slug);
  if (!post || post.data.draft) return null;
  return post;
}

/**
 * Ambil semua slug artikel published — untuk getStaticPaths.
 */
export async function getAllPostSlugs(): Promise<string[]> {
  const posts = await getPublishedPosts();
  return posts.map(p => p.id);
}

/**
 * Format tanggal ke format Indonesia.
 * Contoh: "15 Februari 2026"
 */
export function formatDate(date: Date): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(date);
}

/**
 * Format tanggal singkat.
 * Contoh: "15 Feb 2026"
 */
export function formatDateShort(date: Date): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(date);
}

/**
 * Ambil artikel terkait berdasarkan tag yang sama.
 * Return max `limit` artikel, exclude artikel itu sendiri.
 */
export function getRelatedPosts(
  current: BlogEntry,
  all: BlogEntry[],
  limit = 3
): BlogEntry[] {
  const currentTags = new Set(current.data.tags);

  if (currentTags.size === 0) return [];

  return all
    .filter(post => post.id !== current.id)
    .map(post => ({
      post,
      score: post.data.tags.filter(t => currentTags.has(t)).length,
    }))
    .filter(({ score }) => score > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score)
    .slice(0, limit)
    .map(({ post }) => post);
}

/**
 * Ambil semua tag unik dari artikel published + jumlah per tag.
 * Sort by count descending, lalu alfabetis.
 */
export function collectTags(
  posts: BlogEntry[]
): { tag: string; count: number }[] {
  const counts: Record<string, number> = {};

  posts.forEach(post => {
    post.data.tags.forEach(tag => {
      counts[tag] = (counts[tag] || 0) + 1;
    });
  });

  return Object.entries(counts)
    .map(([tag, count]) => ({ tag, count }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count || a.tag.localeCompare(b.tag, 'id-ID'));
}

/**
 * Ambil artikel by tag.
 */
export function filterByTag(posts: BlogEntry[], tag: string): BlogEntry[] {
  return posts.filter(post => post.data.tags.includes(tag));
}

/**
 * Ambil artikel by category.
 */
export function filterByCategory(
  posts: BlogEntry[],
  category: string
): BlogEntry[] {
  return posts.filter(post => post.data.category === category);
}

/**
 * Build canonical URL untuk artikel blog.
 */
export function postUrl(slug: string): string {
  return `/blog/${slug}/`;
}
===== FILE: src/lib/dom.ts =====
// src/lib/dom.ts
export function cloneTemplate(id: string): HTMLElement | null {
  const tpl = document.getElementById(id) as HTMLTemplateElement | null;
  if (!tpl) return null;
  const frag = tpl.content.cloneNode(true) as DocumentFragment;
  return frag.firstElementChild as HTMLElement | null;
}

===== FILE: src/lib/format.ts =====
const TZ = 'Asia/Jakarta';

const long = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
  day: 'numeric',
  month: 'long',
  year: 'numeric',
  timeZone: TZ,
});

const short = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
  day: 'numeric',
  month: 'short',
  year: 'numeric',
  timeZone: TZ,
});

export function formatDate(date: Date): string {
  return long.format(date);
}

export function formatDateShort(date: Date): string {
  return short.format(date);
}

/** "slice-of-life" -> "Slice Of Life" */
export function genreLabel(slug: string): string {
  return slug
    .split('-')
    .filter(Boolean)
    .map((w) => w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1))
    .join(' ');
}
===== FILE: src/lib/novel.ts =====
import { getCollection, type CollectionEntry } from 'astro:content';

export type SeriesEntry = CollectionEntry<'series'>;
export type ChapterEntry = CollectionEntry<'chapters'>;

export interface ChapterInfo {
  seriesSlug: string;
  number: number;
  title: string;
  publishedAt: Date;
  entry: ChapterEntry;
}

export interface SeriesInfo {
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  author: string;
  cover: string;
  status: string;
  synopsis: string;
  genres: string[];
  year: number | null;
  chapters: ChapterInfo[];
  totalChapters: number;
  latestChapter: number;
  updatedAt: Date;
}

let cache: Promise<SeriesInfo[]> | null = null;

async function build(): Promise<SeriesInfo[]> {
  const [seriesEntries, chapterEntries] = await Promise.all([
    getCollection('series', ({ data }) => !data.draft),
    getCollection('chapters', ({ data }) => !data.draft),
  ]);

  const bySlug = new Map<string, ChapterInfo[]>();
  for (const e of chapterEntries) {
    const slug = e.id.split('/')[0]!;
    const list = bySlug.get(slug) ?? [];
    list.push({
      seriesSlug: slug,
      number: e.data.chapter,
      title: e.data.chapterTitle ?? `Chapter ${e.data.chapter}`,
      publishedAt: e.data.publishedAt,
      entry: e,
    });
    bySlug.set(slug, list);
  }

  return seriesEntries.map((s) => {
    const slug = s.id.split('/')[0]!;
    const chapters = (bySlug.get(slug) ?? []).sort((a, b) => a.number - b.number);

    const seen = new Set<number>();
    for (const c of chapters) {
      if (seen.has(c.number)) {
        throw new Error(`[novel] Chapter ${c.number} duplikat di "${slug}"`);
      }
      seen.add(c.number);
    }

    const latest = chapters.at(-1);
    return {
      slug,
      title: s.data.title,
      author: s.data.author,
      cover: s.data.cover,
      status: s.data.status,
      synopsis: s.data.synopsis,
      genres: s.data.genres,
      year: s.data.year ?? null,
      chapters,
      totalChapters: chapters.length,
      latestChapter: latest?.number ?? 0,
      updatedAt: latest?.publishedAt ?? new Date(0),
    };
  });
}

export function getAllSeries(): Promise<SeriesInfo[]> {
  return (cache ??= build());
}

export async function getSeriesBySlug(slug: string) {
  return (await getAllSeries()).find((s) => s.slug === slug) ?? null;
}

export async function getChaptersBySeries(slug: string) {
  return (await getSeriesBySlug(slug))?.chapters ?? [];
}

export function getAdjacent(chapters: ChapterInfo[], current: number) {
  const i = chapters.findIndex((c) => c.number === current);
  return {
    prev: i > 0 ? chapters[i - 1]! : null,
    next: i >= 0 && i < chapters.length - 1 ? chapters[i + 1]! : null,
  };
}

export async function getGenres() {
  const counts = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const s of await getAllSeries()) {
    for (const g of s.genres) counts.set(g, (counts.get(g) ?? 0) + 1);
  }
  return [...counts]
    .map(([slug, count]) => ({ slug, count }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count || a.slug.localeCompare(b.slug));
}
===== FILE: src/lib/pagination.ts =====
// src/lib/pagination.ts
export function buildPages(current: number, total: number): (number | '...')[] {
  if (total <= 7) {
    return Array.from({ length: total }, (_, i) => i + 1);
  }
  const pages: (number | '...')[] = [1];
  if (current > 3) pages.push('...');
  const start = Math.max(2, current - 1);
  const end = Math.min(total - 1, current + 1);
  for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) pages.push(i);
  if (current < total - 2) pages.push('...');
  pages.push(total);
  return pages;
}

export function paginateList<T>(list: T[], page: number, perPage: number) {
  const total = list.length;
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / perPage));
  const currentPage = Math.min(Math.max(1, page), totalPages);
  const start = (currentPage - 1) * perPage;
  const items = list.slice(start, start + perPage);
  return { items, total, totalPages, currentPage };
}

      </div>

      <div class="detail__head">
        <Badge variant={variant} size="sm">{series.status.toUpperCase()}</Badge>
        <h1 class="detail__title">{series.title}</h1>
        <dl class="meta">
          <div>
            <dt>Penulis</dt>
            <dd>{series.author}</dd>
          </div>
          {series.year && (
            <div>
              <dt>Tahun</dt>
              <dd>{series.year}</dd>
            </div>
          )}
          <div>
            <dt>Chapter</dt>
            <dd>{series.totalChapters}</dd>
          </div>
          {latest && (
            <div>
              <dt>Update</dt>
              <dd>
                <time datetime={latest.publishedAt.toISOString()}>
                  {formatDate(latest.publishedAt)}
                </time>
              </dd>
            </div>
          )}
        </dl>
      </div>

      <div class="detail__body">
        {first ? (
          <div class="actions">
            <LinkButton
              href={URLS.novelChapter(series.slug, first.number)}
              variant="accent"
              size="lg"
              icon="book"
            >
              Mulai Baca
            </LinkButton>
            <LinkButton
              href="#"
              variant="outline"
              size="lg"
              icon="history"
              hidden
              data-continue
            >
              Lanjutkan
            </LinkButton>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <p class="muted">Belum ada chapter.</p>
        )}

        {series.genres.length > 0 && (
          <ul class="genres" aria-label="Genre">
            {series.genres.map((g) => (
              <li>
                <a href={URLS.genre(g)} class="genre-chip">{genreLabel(g)}</a>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}

        {plainSynopsis && (
          <div class="synopsis">
            <SectionLabel as="h2" size="md">Sinopsis</SectionLabel>
            <p>{series.synopsis}</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="chapters" data-chapters data-slug={series.slug}>
      <header class="chapters__header">
        <SectionLabel as="h2" size="md">
          Daftar Chapter
        </SectionLabel>
        {series.totalChapters > 1 && (
          <button
            type="button"
            class="sort-btn"
            data-sort
            aria-label="Ubah urutan chapter"
          >
            Terbaru dulu
          </button>
        )}
      </header>

      {series.totalChapters > 0 ? (
        <ol class="chapter-list" data-list data-order="desc">
          {newestFirst.map((c) => (
            <li>
              <a href={URLS.novelChapter(series.slug, c.number)} data-chapter={c.number}>
                <span class="chapter-no">Ch. {c.number}</span>
                <span class="chapter-name">{chapterName(c)}</span>
                <time class="chapter-date" datetime={c.publishedAt.toISOString()}>
                  {formatDateShort(c.publishedAt)}
                </time>
              </a>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      ) : (
        <p class="muted">Chapter akan segera hadir.</p>
      )}
    </section>
  </Page>
</BaseLayout>

<script>
  const section = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-chapters]');
  const list = section?.querySelector<HTMLOListElement>('[data-list]');

  if (section && list) {
    const slug = section.dataset.slug ?? '';
    const sortBtn = section.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-sort]');

    sortBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const next = list.dataset.order === 'desc' ? 'asc' : 'desc';
      list.dataset.order = next;
      list.append(...Array.from(list.children).reverse());
      sortBtn.textContent = next === 'desc' ? 'Terbaru dulu' : 'Terlama dulu';
    });

    const btn = document.querySelector<HTMLAnchorElement>('[data-continue]');

    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(`yukionovel:last:${slug}`);
      const parsed = raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as { n?: unknown } | null) : null;
      const n = parsed && typeof parsed.n === 'number' ? parsed.n : null;
      const target =
        n !== null
          ? list.querySelector<HTMLAnchorElement>(`a[data-chapter="${n}"]`)
          : null;

      if (btn && target) {
        btn.href = target.href;
        const label = btn.querySelector('.btn-label');
        if (label) label.textContent = `Lanjutkan Ch. ${n}`;
        btn.hidden = false;
        target.closest('li')?.classList.add('is-last');
      }
    } catch {}
  }
</script>

    <Hero title="Semua Novel" />

    {sorted.length > 0 ? (
      <div class="novel-grid">
        {sorted.map((s) => (
          <NovelCard
            novel={{
              id: s.slug,
              data: {
                title: s.title,
                cover: s.cover,
                status: s.status,
                author: s.author,
                synopsis: s.synopsis,
                chapters: Array.from({ length: s.totalChapters }, (_, i) => ({ number: i + 1 })),
              },
            }}
          />
        ))}
      </div>

===== FILE: src/pages/rss.xml.ts =====
import rss from '@astrojs/rss';
import type { APIContext } from 'astro';
import { SITE } from '~/constants';
import { getPublishedPosts } from '~/lib/blog';

export async function GET(context: APIContext) {
  const posts = await getPublishedPosts();

  return rss({
    title: SITE.name,
    description: SITE.description,
    site: context.site!,
    items: posts.map(post => ({
      title: post.data.title,
      description: post.data.description,
      link: `/blog/${post.id}/`,
      pubDate: post.data.date,
      categories: [post.data.category, ...post.data.tags],
      author: post.data.author,
    })),
    customData: `<language>id-ID</language>`,
  });
}

import Icon from './Icons.astro';

type IconName = Parameters<typeof Icon>[0]['name'];

interface Props {
  type?: 'button' | 'submit' | 'reset';
  variant?: 'accent' | 'outline' | 'ghost' | 'gold' | 'danger';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  icon?: IconName;
  iconPos?: 'prefix' | 'suffix';
  block?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  ariaLabel?: string;
  id?: string;
}

const {
  type = 'button',
  variant = 'accent',
  size = 'md',
  icon,
  iconPos = 'prefix',
  block = false,
  disabled = false,
  ariaLabel,
  id,
} = Astro.props;
---

<button
  type={type}
  id={id}
  class:list={[
    'btn',
    `btn-${variant}`,
    `btn-${size}`,
    { 'btn-block': block },
  ]}
  disabled={disabled}
  aria-label={ariaLabel}
>
  {icon && iconPos === 'prefix' && <Icon name={icon} />}
  <span class="btn-label"><slot /></span>
  {icon && iconPos === 'suffix' && <Icon name={icon} />}
</button>

<style>

import Icon from './Icons.astro';

type IconName = Parameters<typeof Icon>[0]['name'];

interface Props {
  href?: string;
  variant?: 'accent' | 'outline' | 'ghost' | 'gold';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  icon?: IconName;
  iconPos?: 'prefix' | 'suffix';
  external?: boolean;
  block?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  hidden?: boolean;
  ariaLabel?: string;
  class?: string;
  [key: string]: any;
}

const {
  href = '#',
  variant = 'accent',
  size = 'md',
  icon,
  iconPos = 'prefix',
  external = false,
  block = false,
  disabled = false,
  hidden = false,
  ariaLabel,
  class: className,
  ...rest
} = Astro.props;

const Tag = disabled ? 'span' : 'a';
---

<Tag
  href={disabled ? undefined : href}
  class:list={[
    'link-btn',
    `btn-${variant}`,
    `btn-${size}`,
    {
      'btn-block': block,
      'link-btn--disabled': disabled,
    },
    className,
  ]}
  aria-label={ariaLabel}
  aria-disabled={disabled ? 'true' : undefined}
  hidden={hidden}
  target={!disabled && external ? '_blank' : undefined}
  rel={!disabled && external ? 'noopener noreferrer' : undefined}
  {...rest}
>
  {icon && iconPos === 'prefix' && <Icon name={icon} />}
  <span class="btn-label"><slot /></span>
  {icon && iconPos === 'suffix' && <Icon name={icon} />}
</Tag>

  .card-pad-none { padding: 0; }

  .card-pad-md   { padding: var(--s-4); }
  .card-pad-lg   { padding: var(--s-6); }

  .card-lift {
    transition: transform var(--t-fast), border-color var(--t-fast), background var(--t-fast);
  }
  .card-lift:hover {
    transform: translateY(-2px);
  }
</style>
